| Agreement | The following TP for TS 38.212 Clause 6.3.1.1.2 is endorsed.<br>The corresponding final CR for Rel-19 TS 38.212 in R1-2603378 is endorsed<br>Reason for change: For P/SP CSI reporting on PUCCH for performance monitoring, it is possible that the number of information bits of RS-PAI becomes less than 3 bits. However, NR specifications do not support PUCCH carrying 1 or 2 information bits other than HARQ-ACK/SR.<br>Summary of change: For P/SP CSI reporting on PUCCH for performance monitoring, the number of information bits of related CSI is zero padded to 3 bits.<br>Consequences if not approved: For P/SP CSI reporting on PUCCH for performance monitoring, 1-bit or 2-bit payload size of RS-PAI is not supported.<br>\| 6.3.1.1.2 CSI only <omitted texts> If none of the CSI reports for transmission on a PUCCH is of two parts, the CSI fields of all CSI reports, in the order from upper part to lower part in Table 6.3.1.1.2-12, are mapped to the UCI bit sequence starting with . The most significant bit of each field is mapped to the lowest order information bit for that field, e.g. the most significant bit of the first field is mapped to. If the length of UCI bit sequence is less than 3 bits, zeros shall be appended to the UCI bit sequence until its length equals 3. Table 6.3.1.1.2-12: Mapping order of CSI reports to UCI bit sequence , without two-part CSI report(s) <omitted texts> \| | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2603260](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2603260.zip)<br>`[124b-R19-AI/ML]` |

| Agreement | The following changes in Section 7.5 in TS38.213 are endorsed.<br>The corresponding CR for Rel-19 TS38.213 in R1-2603403 is endorsed.<br>- Reason for change: For UL power prioritization, the case of multiplexing UEIRI in a PUSCH transmission is not captured in current specifications which lead to unclear UE behaviour.<br>- Summary of change: Reuse the rule for multiplexing HARQ-ACK in a PUSCH transmission for the case of multiplexing UEIRI in a PUSCH transmission.<br>- Consequences if not approved: Unclear UE behaviour.<br>\| 7.5 Prioritizations for transmission power reductions For single cell operation with two uplink carriers or for operation with carrier aggregation or for operation with a candidate cell configured by LTM-Config or for operation with a single cell configured with sTx-2Panel, if a total UE transmit power for PUSCH or PUCCH or PRACH or SRS transmissions on serving cell(s) or on a candidate cell, if any, in a frequency range in a respective transmission occasion would exceed , where is the linear value of in transmission occasion as defined in [8-1, TS 38.101-1] for FR1 and [8-2, TS 38.101-2] for FR2, the UE allocates power to PUSCH/PUCCH/PRACH/SRS transmissions according to the following priority order (in descending order) so that the total UE transmit power for transmissions on serving cell(s) or on a candidate cell, if any, in the frequency range is smaller than or equal to for that frequency range in every symbol of transmission occasion . If the UE transmits SRS on multiple SRS resources according to Clause 6.2.1.4 of [6, TS 38.214], the UE allocates power so that all REs of the SRS transmission have same power. For the purpose of power allocation in this clause, if a UE is provided uci-MuxWithDiffPrio and the UE multiplexes HARQ-ACK information in a PUSCH, a priority index of the PUSCH is the larger of (a) the priority index of the PUSCH according to clause 9 and (b) the larger priority index of the HARQ-ACK information. When determining a total transmit power for serving cells or a candidate cell, if any, as described in Clause 21 in a frequency range in a symbol of transmission occasion , the UE does not include power for transmissions starting after the symbol of transmission occasion . The total UE transmit power in a symbol of a slot is defined as the sum of the linear values of UE transmit powers for PUSCH, PUCCH, PRACH, and SRS in the symbol of the slot. - PRACH transmission on a candidate cell, if any, as described in Clause 21 - PRACH transmission on the PCell - PUCCH or PUSCH transmissions with larger priority index - For PUCCH or PUSCH transmissions with same priority index - PUCCH transmission with HARQ-ACK information, and/or SR, and/or LRR, and/or UEIRI, or PUSCH transmission with HARQ-ACK information and/or UEIRI of the priority index - PUCCH transmission with CSI or PUSCH transmission with CSI - PUSCH transmission without HARQ-ACK information and UEIRI of the priority index or CSI and, for Type-2 random access procedure, PUSCH transmission on the PCell - If the UE is configured with prioSCellPRACH-OverSP-PeriodicSRS-r17 - Aperiodic SRS transmission or PRACH transmission on a serving cell other than the PCell - Semi-persistent and/or periodic SRS transmission - otherwise, - SRS transmission, with aperiodic SRS having higher priority than semi-persistent and/or periodic SRS, or PRACH transmission on a serving cell other than the PCell <Unchanged parts are omitted> \| | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2603249](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2603249.zip)<br>`[124-R19b-MIMO]` |

| Agreement | The following TP for Clause 5.2.1.5.4.1 in Rel-19 TS38.214 is endorsed.<br>The corresponding final CR for Rel-19 TS38.214 in R1-2603465 is endorsed.<br>- Reason for change: Current specification for Mode B UE-initiated reporting defines the timing only with reference to the first transmitted PUCCH. When UEIRI is multiplexed on a PUSCH, the processing time may not be enough for the network for rescheduling the CG PUSCH resource.<br>- Summary of change: Clarify that for the determination of type 1 CG-PUSCH, “PUCCH” is replaced with “PUSCH with UEIRI” if the UE would multiplex UEIRI in a PUSCH.<br>- Consequences if not approved: If this ambiguity is not resolved, different implementations may interpret the reference point differently, leading to inconsistent UE behavior and potential interoperability issues in Mode B reporting.<br>\| 5.2.1.5.4.1 UE Initiated CSI reporting For a UE configured with a CSI-ReportConfig with eventType for periodic reference signals with the same periodicity configured by the newBeamResourceSet, without eventDetectionTimeWindowLength, and with dl-OrJointTCI-StateList, when an event instance is determined (as described in the following clauses), - for a reference signal configured by the newBeamResourceSet, if eventType is set to ‘event2’ or ‘event7’, or - for the reference signal in the indicated TCI state or the SS/PBCH block which is QCLed with the reference signal in the indicated TCI state, if eventType is set to ‘event1’, the UE transmits UEIRI on a PUCCH format 0 or format 1 in the PUCCH resource (in the CC provided by pucchCell, if configured, in the CSI-ReportConfig) configured by PUCCHResource in the CSI-ReportConfig. For a UE configured with a CSI-ReportConfig with eventType for periodic reference signals with the same periodicity configured by the newBeamResourceSet, with eventDetectionTimeWindowLength, and with dl-OrJointTCI-StateList, when an event instance is determined (as described in the following clauses), - for a reference signal configured by the newBeamResourceSet, if eventType is set to ‘event2’ or ‘event7’, or - for the reference signal in the indicated TCI state or the SS/PBCH block which is QCLed with the reference signal in the indicated TCI state, if eventType is set to ‘event1’, the UE - starts a timer for such reference signal from the initial value equal to the time window length provided by eventDetectionTimeWindowLength and sets the counter to 1, if the timer for such reference signal is not running; or - increments the counter for such reference signal by 1, if the timer for such reference signal is running. If the number of event instances determined by the counter for such reference signal is greater than or equal to eventInstanceCount, the UE transmits UEIRI on a PUCCH format 0 or format 1 in the PUCCH resource (in the CC provided by pucchCell, if configured, in the CSI-ReportConfig) configured by PUCCHResource in the CSI-ReportConfig. The counter of the event instances for such reference signal is reset: - if the reference signal in the indicated TCI state or the SS/PBCH block which is QCLed with the reference signal in the indicated TCI state is updated when the UE is configured with a CSI-ReportConfig with the higher layer parameter eventType set to ‘event2’ or ‘event1’, or - if the reference signal with the valueOfQ-th highest L1-RSRP out of the reference signals among the activated TCI states or the SS/PBCH block with the valueOfQ-th highest L1-RSRP out of the SS/PBCH blocks QCLed with the reference signals among the activated TCI states, which determines event instance(s), is updated when the UE is configured with a CSI-ReportConfig with the higher layer parameter eventType set to ‘event7’, or - if eventThreshold or valueOfQ or eventInstanceCount or eventDetectionTimeWindow in the CSI-ReportConfig are reconfigured. The UE does not expect that a CSI trigger state associated with CSI report configuration(s) configured with the higher layer parameter eventType is further associated with other CSI report configurations that are not configured with the higher layer parameter eventType. In the remaining of this clause, for the determination of type 1 CG-PUSCH configured by configuredPUSCHResourceOfModeB, “PUCCH” is replaced with “PUSCH with UEIRI” if the UE would multiplex UEIRI in a PUSCH [6, TS38.213]. \| | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2603250](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2603250.zip)<br>`[124-R19b-MIMO]` |

Summary of change: It is clarified that when PRACH collides with SSB whose periodicity has been adapted, the R19 UE will not transmit PRACH Consequence if not approved: The UE may attempt to transmit a PRACH even though the PRACH can be overlapped with gNB’s SSB transmission. \|<br>\| 8.1 Random access preamble < Unchanged parts are omitted > For unpaired spectrum, - if a UE is not provided tdd-UL-DL-ConfigurationCommon for a cell, a PRACH occasion for the cell in a PRACH slot is valid if it does not precede a SS/PBCH block in the PRACH slot and starts at least symbols after a last SS/PBCH block reception symbol, where is provided in Table 8.1-2 and, if channelAccessMode = "semiStatic" is provided, does not overlap with a set of consecutive symbols before the start of a next channel occupancy time where the UE does not transmit [15, TS 37.213] - the candidate SS/PBCH block index of the SS/PBCH block corresponds to the SS/PBCH block index provided by ssb-PositionsInBurst in SIB1 or in ServingCellConfigCommon or in SSB-MTC-AdditionalPCI corresponding to the cell, as described in clause 4.1 - for each of the candidate cells configured by LTM-Config, if a UE is not provided ltm-TDD-UL-DL-ConfigurationCommon, a PRACH occasion in a PRACH slot for a candidate cell is valid if it does not precede a SS/PBCH block in the PRACH slot and starts at least symbols after a last SS/PBCH block reception symbol, where is provided in Table 8.1-2 - the SS/PBCH block index of the SS/PBCH block corresponds to the SS/PBCH block index provided by ssb-PositionsInBurst in LTM-SSB-Config corresponding to the candidate cell - if a UE is provided tdd-UL-DL-ConfigurationCommon or sib1-tdd-UL-DL-ConfigurationCommon for a cell, a PRACH occasion for the cell in a PRACH slot is valid if - for a first PRACH occasion - it is within UL symbols, or - it does not precede a SS/PBCH block in the PRACH slot and starts at least symbols after a last downlink symbol and at least symbols after a last SS/PBCH block symbol, where is provided in Table 8.1-2, and if channelAccessMode = "semiStatic" is provided, does not overlap with a set of consecutive symbols before the start of a next channel occupancy time where there shall not be any transmissions, as described in [15, TS 37.213] - the candidate SS/PBCH block index of the SS/PBCH block corresponds to the SS/PBCH block index provided by ssb-PositionsInBurst in SIB1 or in ServingCellConfigCommon or in SSB-MTC-AdditionalPCI corresponding to the cell, as described in clause 4.1 - for a second PRACH occasion - it is in RBs that are both in the active UL BWP and in the UL sub-band, and - it starts at least symbols after a last non-SBFD downlink symbol and at least symbols after a last SS/PBCH block symbol, where is provided in Table 8.1-2, and does not overlap with a SS/PBCH block symbol, and - it is only within SBFD symbols, includes at least one SBFD symbol indicated as downlink by tdd-UL-DL-ConfigurationCommon, if the UE is provided sbfd-RACHSingleConfig, or - it is only within SBFD symbols, if the UE is provided sbfd-RACHDualConfig, or - it starts from an SBFD symbol and ends in a non-SBFD symbols, if the UE is provided sbfd-RACHDualConfig and sbfd-RACHDualConfig-ValidROAcrossSymbolTypes - for each of the candidate cells configured by LTM-config, if a UE is provided ltm-tdd-UL-DL-ConfigurationCommon, a PRACH occasion in a PRACH slot for a candidate cell is valid if - it is within UL symbols, or - it does not precede a SS/PBCH block in the PRACH slot and starts at least symbols after a last downlink symbol and at least symbols after a last SS/PBCH block symbol, where is provided in Table 8.1-2 - the SS/PBCH block index of the SS/PBCH block corresponds to the SS/PBCH block index provided by ssb-PositionsInBurst in LTM-SSB-Config corresponding to the candidate cell. - if a UE has received indication for adaptation of periodicity of SS/PBCH block, as described in Clause 11.6 and a PRACH occasion for the cell in a PRACH slot is valid, UE does not transmit PRACH on the valid PRACH occasion if the PRACH occasion precedes an SS/PBCH block in the PRACH slot and does not start at least symbols after a last SS/PBCH block symbol with the adapted periodicity, where is provided in Table 8.1-2. < Unchanged parts are omitted > In the following, for determining valid PRACH occasions, if a UE is not provided SS/PBCH block indexes by ssb-PositionsInBurst in ServingCellConfigCommon and is provided OD-SSB-Config, reference to SS/PBCH blocks is for the union of SS/PBCH blocks provided by each combination of od-SSB-SFN-Offset, od-SSB-HalfFrameIndex, od-SSB-PositionsInBurst and od-SSB-Periodicity in OD-SSB-Config. If the UE is provided SS/PBCH block indexes by ssb-PositionsInBurst in ServingCellConfigCommon and is provided OD-SSB-Config, reference to SS/PBCH blocks is for the union of SS/PBCH blocks provided by ssb-Periodicity and ssb-PositionsInBurst in ServingCellConfigCommon and by each combination of od-SSB-SFN-Offset, od-SSB-HalfFrameIndex, od-SSB-PositionsInBurst and od-SSB-Periodicity in OD-SSB-Config. If a UE is provided SS/PBCH block indexes by ssb-PositionsInBurst in ServingCellConfigCommon and is provided ssb-BurstPeriodicityList, reference to SS/PBCH blocks is based on the periodicity provided by ssb-PeriodicityServingCell in ServingCellConfigCommon. \| | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2603359](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2603359.zip)<br>`[124b-R19-NES]` |

| Agreement | Adopt the following TP to section 10.4D, TS 38.213 as alignment CR:<br>\| ---------------------------------Start of TP----------------------- 10.4D PDCCH monitoring activation by WUS in RRC_CONNECTED <Unchanged parts are omitted> A UE can be provided by lpwus-Mo12 a periodicity, by periodicityMO-Option 1-2, and a time offset, by offsetMO-Option 1-2, relative to the start of a system frame with SFN 0, for the UE to determine the first WUS monitoring occasions from a number of WUS monitoring occasions per periodicity, provided by lpwus-NumOfMo12. <Unchanged parts are omitted> ---------------------------------End of TP----------------------- \| | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602036](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602036.zip)<br>`[124b-R19-LP-WUS]` |
| Agreement | Adopt the following TP to section 10.4D, TS 38.213 as alignment CR:<br>\| ======================== Start of TP======================================= 10.4D PDCCH monitoring activation by WUS in RRC_CONNECTED A UE configured with DRX mode operation and operating in the RRC_CONNECTED state can be provided for WUS reception on the primary cell of a cell group - a number of OOK symbols per OFDM symbol, a first RB by lpwus-StartRB, and overlaid OFDM sequences per OOK symbol for WUS reception [4, TS 38.211], and - a number of codepoints provided for the UE by the WUS [6, TS 38.212], by lpwus-CodepointWUS-codepointCONNECTED A UE assumes that a WUS is quasi co-located with an SS/PBCH block or a CSI-RS with respect to quasi co-location ‘typeC’ or ‘typeD’ properties, when applicable. If a UE is provided dl-OrJointTCI-StateList, the UE receives WUS based on the quasi co-location information of the TCI states provided by dl-OrJointTCI-StateList, indicated by a most recent DCI format or MAC CE, after a respective application time in clause 5.1.5 [6, TS 38.214]; otherwise, the UE receives WUS based on the quasi co-location information of the TCI states for a CORESET with controlResourceSetId value that is same as the one indicated by lpwus-TCI-StatesWUS_TCI_states_CONNECTED. A UE assumes that an SCS configuration for WUS receptions is same as an SCS configuration for the active DL BWP. A UE does not monitor a WUS during Active Time [11, TS 38.321]. A UE does not monitor WUS during DTX inactive period for the primary cell. When lpwus-PDCCH-MonitoringTimer is not provided, A a UE can be provided by lpwus-MO-1-1WUS-MOCONNECTED-Option1-1 a periodicity, by periodicityMO-Option 1-1, and a time offset, by offsetMO-Option 1-1, relative to the start of a system frame with SFN 0, for the UE to determine WUS monitoring occasions. The UE starts to monitor WUS in a first WUS monitoring occasion that is not earlier than a first slot that is prior to a second slot where the drx-onDurationTimer would start by a time provided by lpwus-TimeOffset1-1timeOffsetCONNECTEDOption1-1, and monitors WUS for a number of monitoring occasions provided by lpwus-NumOfMO-1-1numMO-Option 1-1. The UE reports a number of milliseconds [18, TS 38.306] where the UE is not required to monitor WUS prior to the slot where the drx-onDurationTimer would start. The UE is not required to monitor WUS within the reported number of slots prior to the slot where the drx-onDurationTimer would start. If the UE determines to monitor PDCCH based on a detected WUS, the UE starts the drx-onDurationTimer [11, TS 38.321]. When lpwus-PDCCH-MonitoringTimer is provided, aA UE can be provided by lpwus-MO-1-2WUS-MOCONNECTED-Option1-2 a periodicity, by periodicityMO-Option 1-2, and a time offset, by offsetMO-Option 1-2, relative to the start of a system frame with SFN 0, for the UE to determine first WUS monitoring occasions from a number of WUS monitoring occasions per periodicity, provided by lpwus-NumOfMO-1-2numMO-perPeriodicity-Option 1-2. The UE assumes WUS monitoring occasions per periodicity are consecutive in time. The UE reports a number of milliseconds [18, TS 38.306] and expects that a time gap, from a last WUS monitoring occasion from the number of WUS monitoring occasions per periodicity to the slot where the lpwus-PDCCH-MonitoringTimer would start, is no smaller than the reported number of milliseconds If the UE determines to monitor PDCCH based on a detected WUS, the UE starts lpwus-PDCCH-MonitoringTimer [11, TS 38.321] after a time, provided by lpwus-TimeOffset1-2timeOffsetCONNECTEDOption1-2, with respect to the start of the first WUS monitoring occasion from the number of WUS monitoring occasions per periodicity. A UE can be provided, by lpwus-AvailableSlot in LPWUS-ConfigWUS_available_slot_CONNECTED, a bitmap that corresponds to a set of time units that repeats continuously and indicates a subset of time units from the set of time units that is available for the UE to monitor WUS [12, TS 38.331]. A time unit includes one slot or two slots. A set of time units includes a total of either 10, or 20, or 40 time units. A duration , in msec, of the set of time units has maximum value of 40 msec. The first symbol of the set of time units every 40 msec/ periods is a first symbol in frame mod 4 = 0. The UE can be additionally provided, by lpwus-AvailableSymbol in LPWUS-ConfigWUS_available_symbol_CONNECTED, an indication of symbols in each time unit from the subset of time units that is available for the UE to monitor WUS. If the UE is not provided lpwus-AvailableSlotWUS_available_slot_CONNECTED, the UE assumes that all time units are available for the UE to monitor WUS. If the UE is not provided lpwus-AvailableSymbolWUS_available_symbol_CONNECTED, the UE assumes a time unit of one slot and, for a time unit that is available for the UE to monitor WUS, all symbols in the time unit are available for the UE to monitor WUS. The UE assumes that a symbol is not available to monitor WUS when - the symbol is indicated as uplink, by tdd-UL-DL-configurationCommon or tdd-UL-DL-ConfigurationDedicated - the symbol is indicated for transmission of SS/PBCH blocks, by ssb-PositionsInBurst in SIB1 or in ServingCellConfigCommon A WUS monitoring occasion is over a first number of symbols, provided by lpwus-NominalMoDuration in LPWUS-ConfigWUS_NominalMO_duration_CONNECTED. If a number of available symbols for the UE to monitor WUS in a WUS monitoring occasion is smaller than a second number of symbols, provided by lpwus-ActualDuration in LPWUS-ConfigWUS_ActualMO_duration_CONNECTED, the UE does not monitor WUS in the WUS monitoring occasion. The UE monitors WUS in a WUS monitoring occasion over the earliest available lpwus-ActualDurationWUS_ActualMO_duration_CONNECTED symbols in the WUS monitoring occasion. If a UE detects a codepoint in a WUS reception, from the number of codepoints, on the primary cell of the cell group, the UE starts monitoring PDCCH on all applicable serving cells of the cell group, and the UE does not need to monitor WUS before lpwus-PDCCH-MonitoringTimer starts, if lpwus-PDCCH-MonitoringTimer is provided. ========================= End of TP======================================= \|<br>R1-263402	Summary #2 of maintenance on Low-power wake-up signal and receiver for NR (LP-WUS/WUR)			Moderator (vivo) | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602036](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602036.zip)<br>`[124b-R19-LP-WUS]` |

Consequences if not approved: UE’s behaviors of CSI acquisition for a candidate cell become incomplete. \|<br>\| 5.2.4a CSI Reporting for LTM and handover <Unchanged parts are omitted> After a UE receives an LTM Cell Switch Command MAC CE [10, TS 38.321] providing a candidate cell (given by Target Configuration ID field), and a ltm-CSI-ReportConfig is configured for the candidate cell, the UE can measure corresponding NZP CSI-RS resources and CSI-IM resources if configured, and shall transmit a CSI report to the candidate cell. After a UE receives a ReconfigurationWithSync configured with earlyCSI-Acquisition, the UE can measure corresponding NZP CSI-RS resources and CSI-IM resources if configured, and shall transmit a CSI report to the target cell. <Unchanged parts are omitted> If a valid CSI is not available, the UE shall transmit a CSI report which contains a CQI corresponding to the lowest CQI index. | Agreement | - Adopt the following TP to TS38.214 Section 5.2.4a<br>\| Reason for change: In existing specification, for early CSI acquisition during RACH-based HO with CBRA, the current 2-step RA relies on “after MsgB HARQ-ACK”, which does not cover the MsgA success path via C-RNTI addressed PDCCH UL grant, and it also does not exclude the NACK case. Summary of change: Update TS 38.214 to clarify that ACK value is required for the MsgB HARQ-ACK and allow the first PUSCH to be the one scheduled by a C-RNTI addressed PDCCH received in response to MsgA. Consequences if not approved: Early CSI acquisition for 2-step RA HO may be missing in the MsgA UL-grant success path and may be triggered after a NACK, leading to inconsistent UE behavior and potential performance/interoperability issues. \|<br>\| 5.2.4a CSI Reporting for LTM and handover ==================================unchanged omitted======================= For RACH-less LTM cell switch or RACH-less handover [23, TS 38.300], the UE shall transmit the CSI report to the candidate cell using the first PUSCH corresponding to a dynamic grant or a configured grant [6, TS 38.213]. For RACH-based LTM cell switch or RACH-based handover using a contention-free random access procedure [23, TS 38.300], the UE shall transmit the CSI report to the candidate cell using the PUSCH scheduled by the RAR UL grant or MsgA PUSCH. For RACH-based LTM cell switch or RACH-based handover using a contention-based random access procedure [23, TS 38.300], the UE shall transmit the CSI report to the candidate cell using the first PUSCH corresponding to a dynamic grant or a configured grant after the HARQ-ACK transmission corresponding to MsgB, or the first PUSCH scheduled by a PDCCH transmission addressed to the C-RNTI, received in response to Msg3. ==================================unchanged omitted==================== \|<br>The final CR in R1-2603426 is endorsed. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2603381](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2603381.zip)<br>`[124b-R19-others]` |
| Agreement | - Adopt the following TP to TS38.214 Section 5.2.4a<br>\| Reason for change: \| For CBRA based LTM or handover scenario, UE shall transmit CSI report to the candidate cell using the first PUSCH corresponding to a dynamic grant or a configured grant after the HARQ-ACK transmission corresponding to PDSCH scheduled by a PDCCH transmission addressed to the C-RNTI that is received in response to MSG A. However, the above method for determining UL resource of early CSI report is missing in the RAN1 spec, i.e., TS 38.214 clause 5.2.4a. \|<br>\|  \|  \|<br>\| Summary of change: \| For CBRA based LTM or handover case, add a rule to determine UL resource of early CSI report if C-RNTI MAC CE is included in MSG A, i.e., For RACH-based LTM cell switch or RACH handover using a contention-based random access procedure [23, TS 38.300], the UE shall transmit the CSI report to the candidate cell using the first PUSCH corresponding to a dynamic grant or a configured grant after the HARQ-ACK transmission corresponding to PDSCH scheduled by a PDCCH transmission addressed to the C-RNTI that is received in response to MSG A. \|<br>\|  \|  \|<br>\| Consequences if not approved: \| The UE behavior on determining UL resource of early CSI report when C-RNTI has been included in MSG A for CBRA based LTM or handover scenario is unclear. \|<br>\| 5.2.4a CSI Reporting for LTM and handover *** Unchanged parts are omitted *** For RACH-less LTM cell switch or RACH-less handover [23, TS 38.300], the UE shall transmit the CSI report to the candidate cell using the first PUSCH corresponding to a dynamic grant or a configured grant [6, TS 38.213]. For RACH-based LTM cell switch or RACH-based handover using a contention-free random access procedure [23, TS 38.300], the UE shall transmit the CSI report to the candidate cell using the PUSCH scheduled by the RAR UL grant or MsgA PUSCH. For RACH-based LTM cell switch or RACH-based handover using a contention-based random access procedure [23, TS 38.300], the UE shall transmit the CSI report to the candidate cell using the first PUSCH corresponding to a dynamic grant or a configured grant after the HARQ-ACK transmission corresponding to MsgB, or the first PUSCH scheduled by a PDCCH transmission addressed to the C-RNTI, received in response to Msg3. *** Unchanged parts are omitted *** \|<br>The final CR in R1-2603427 is endorsed. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2603381](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2603381.zip)<br>`[124b-R19-others]` |

| Agreement | For aperiodic CSI-RS associated with aperiodic CSI reporting triggered by MAC-CE in MSG4, down select one from the following options in RAN1#125:<br>- Option-1: The triggered aperiodic CSI-RS is QCLed Type-C with the SSB the UE identified during the random access procedure when UE transition from IDLE/INACTIVE to CONNECTED mode<br>- Option-2: If the aperiodic CSI-RS is triggered along with an aperiodic TRS by the same MAC-CE in MSG4 and the aperiodic TRS is transmitted before the aperiodic CSI-RS , the CSI-RS is QCLed Type-A with the triggered aperiodic TRS; otherwise, the CSI-RS is QCLed Type-C with the SSB the UE identified during the random access procedure when UE transition from IDLE/INACTIVE to CONNECTED mode.<br>    - FFS whether/how there is impact on the time gap requirements between the triggered AP-TRS and AP-CSI-RS.<br>FFS: Whether to consider QCL Type-D when applicable | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602826](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602826.zip)<br>`[124b-R20-MIMO]` |
| Agreement | On how to determine the serving cell(s) triggered with aperiodic SRS via ‘SRS request’ field in a DCI indicating switching out of dormancy based on legacy DCI formats, support Alt-1 with modification, i.e.,<br>- When Rel-20 SRS triggering for SCell switching out of dormancy is enabled, the SRS trigger state indicated in ‘SRS request’ field applies to,<br>  - The SCell(s) which is indicated with switching out of dormancy and configured with aperiodic SRS resource set(s) for SRS-AS associated with the SRS trigger state.<br>  - FFS: The cell(s) scheduled by legacy mechanism and configured with aperiodic SRS resource set(s) associated with the SRS trigger state;<br>  - FFS: When DCI 0_1/1_1 is used for indicating switching out of dormancy, whether only one serving cell is triggered with SRS.<br>    - Note: If only one serving cell is triggered with SRS, Alt 2 can be one of the candidate for DCI 0_1/1_1.<br>  - FFS: How to enable Rel-20 SRS triggering for SCell switching out of dormancy | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602828](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602828.zip)<br>`[124b-R20-MIMO]` |
| Agreement | For CSI-RS frequency-domain density ρ = 1/3, 1/4, 1/6 or 1/8 to the K NZP CSI-RS resources aggregating 48/64/128 CSI-RS ports, support RRC-configured RB-level offset(s) to indicate the occupied RBs of NZP CSI-RS resources.<br>- FFS: Candidate value(s) of the RB-level offset<br>- FFS: Whether to introduce any RB location restriction for the K aggregated NZP CSI-RS resources.<br>- FFS: Whether the RB-level offset(s) is configured per NZP CSI-RS resource or configured per NZP CSI-RS resource set. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602829](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602829.zip)<br>`[124b-R20-MIMO]` |
| Agreement | For K NZP CSI-RS resources for 48/64/128 CSI-RS ports aggregation, configured with density = 1/3, 1/4, 1/6 or 1/8,<br>- Study and decide in RAN1#125 whether to introduce the frequency-domain restriction of the occupied RBs of the NZP CSI-RS resources, e.g.,<br>  - Option-1: The REs occupied by the aggregated NZP CSI-RS resources should be the same as those occupied by one or more ZP CSI-RS resources with frequency density of 1 or 1/2<br>  - Option-2: The aggregated low-frequency-density NZP CSI-RS resources should be allocated in one or two consecutive RBs.<br>  - Option-3: The K aggregated NZP CSI-RS resources are uniformly distributed evenly within every 1/ρ RBs<br>- Study and decide in RAN1#125 whether to follow Rel-19 time-domain restriction, i.e., the K NZP CSI-RS resources should be allocated in one or two consecutive slots | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602829](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602829.zip)<br>`[124b-R20-MIMO]` |
| Conclusion | For early aperiodic SRS when UE transition from IDLE/INACTIVE to CONNECTED mode, there is no RAN1 consensus to support the dynamic indication of the comb offset, cyclic shift via MAC-CE in MSG4, i.e., the comb offset, cyclic shift are configured in SRS configuration(s) provided by SIBx. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602829](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602829.zip)<br>`[124b-R20-MIMO]` |
| Conclusion | Decide in RAN1#125:<br>- Whether to support dynamic indication of the repetition factor of a triggered SRS via MAC-CE in MSG4.<br>- Whether to support dynamic indication of the number of TRS burst(s) via MAC-CE in MSG4. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602829](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602829.zip)<br>`[124b-R20-MIMO]` |
| Agreement | For cross-slot SRS, support PUSCH transmission with a priority index 0 and corresponding DMRS in a serving cell after all the SRS resource(s) of the same resource set with time-domain resource allocation entirely in the U slot in the serving cell. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602528](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602528.zip)<br>`[124b-R20-MIMO]` |
| Agreement | For the time-domain location determination of aperiodic SRS resources within the cross-slot resource set,<br>  - per-resource slot offset of an AP SRS resource is included in the resource configuration. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602528](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602528.zip)<br>`[124b-R20-MIMO]` |
| Conclusion | For intra-repetition hopping for SRS repetition symbols within each SRS frequency hop, whether phase continuity can be maintained is up to UE implementation. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602529](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602529.zip)<br>`[124b-R20-MIMO]` |

## 9.3.1 R2D signals, channels, waveform and procedures

9 Release 20 NR › 9.3 Solutions for Ambient IoT (Internet of Things) in NR Phase 2

| 구분 | 내용 | 출처 |
|---|---|---|
| Conclusion | If no agreement on any change to aspects specified in Rel-19, they are assumed to be supported and reused in Rel-20.<br>- It does not preclude RAN1 can make explicit agreement/conclusion on certain aspects to confirm no change from Rel-19. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602281](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602281.zip) |
| Conclusion | For R2D, RAN1 conclude that the R2D minimum transmission BW associated with M values of {2, 6, 12, 24} are not changed from Rel-19. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602281](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602281.zip) |
| Agreement | For R2D, regarding disabling of FEC and/or block-level repetition,<br>- For periodic broadcast information, support at least one from the following options<br>  - Option 1: FEC and repetitions are always applied<br>  - Option 2: Either or both of FEC and repetitions can be disabled<br>- For L1 R2D control information, support at least one from the following options<br>  - Option 1: FEC and repetitions are always applied<br>  - Option 2: Either or both of FEC and repetitions can be disabled<br>- For PRDCH carrying data payload<br>  - Either or both of FEC and repetitions can be disabled | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602281](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602281.zip) |
| Agreement | For R2D FEC, code rate 1/3 is supported<br>- FFS: code rate 1/2 and 1/4 | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602281](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602281.zip) |
| Agreement | For R2D, repetition number(s) are to be selected from the following candidate values,<br>- {1, 2, 4, 8, 16} | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602282](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602282.zip) |
| Agreement | For R2D FEC, RAN1 further select from the following candidate coding schemes<br>- Coding scheme 1: LTE tail biting convolutional code<br>- Coding scheme 2: Tailed convolutional codes with the same polynomials as LTE tail biting convolutional codes is used for TB size > [200], otherwise LTE tail biting convolutional code is used | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602282](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602282.zip) |
| Agreement | For R2D periodic sync signal, the pre-defined transmission periodicity (Psync) is at least hundreds of milliseconds and to be selected from the following candidate values,<br>- {160ms, 320ms, 640ms}<br>- FFS: whether other values to be considered as candidate values | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602282](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602282.zip) |
| Agreement | For R2D periodic transmission of broadcast information,<br>- For single-step acquisition (if supported), the transmission periodicity (Pbc) is a pre-defined value. The value of Pbc is to be selected from the followings<br>  - Option B1: same as the pre-defined transmission periodicity (Psync) of R2D periodic sync signal<br>  - Option B2: integer multiple of Psync<br>- For two-steps acquisition (if supported),<br>  - For MIB-like broadcast information, the transmission periodicity (Pmib) is a pre-defined value. The value of Pmib is to be selected from the followings<br>    - Option M1: same as the pre-defined transmission periodicity (Psync) of R2D periodic sync signal<br>    - Option M2: integer multiple of Psync<br>  - For SIB1-like broadcast information, the transmission periodicity (Psib) is a pre-defined value or indicated by MIB-like broadcast information. The value of Psib is to be selected from the followings<br>    - Option S1: same as the pre-defined transmission periodicity (Psync) of R2D periodic sync signal<br>    - Option S2: integer multiple of Psync | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602282](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602282.zip) |
| Agreement | For R2D periodic transmission of broadcast information, PRDCH is used to carry broadcast information | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602282](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602282.zip) |
| Working Assumption | For R2D CFO calibration signal, unmodulated single tone sinusoid is supported. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602282](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602282.zip) |
| Agreement | For R2D periodic transmission of broadcast information, the time resource of a broadcast information transmission is to be selected from the followings<br>- For single-step acquisition (if supported),<br>  - Option B1: immediately after a R2D periodic sync signal transmission<br>  - Option B2: after a R2D periodic sync signal transmission and a pre-defined time<br>    - FFS: details regarding the pre-defined time<br>- For two-step acquisition (if supported),<br>  - For MIB-like broadcast information,<br>    - Option M1: immediately after a periodic R2D sync signal transmission<br>    - Option M2: after a periodic R2D sync signal transmission and a pre-defined time<br>      - FFS: details regarding the pre-defined time<br>  - For SIB1-like broadcast information,<br>    - Option S1: explicitly indicated or derived from the indication in MIB-like broadcast information<br>    - Option S2: pre-defined in relation to the time resource of MIB-like broadcast information | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602283](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602283.zip) |
| Agreement | For R2D periodic transmission of broadcast information, at least the following transmission parameters for broadcast information are included<br>- For single-step acquisition (if supported),<br>  - Alt1: there is no L1 R2D control information preceding the transmission of broadcast information (if supported)<br>    - M value is a pre-defined value as M=2<br>    - FEC code rate of periodic broadcast information is a pre-defined value<br>    - Repetition number of periodic broadcast information is a pre-defined value<br>  - Alt2: there is L1 R2D control information preceding the transmission of broadcast information (if supported)<br>    - M value, FEC code rate and repetition number are indicated by the L1 R2D control information<br>- For two-step acquisition (if supported),<br>  - For MIB-like broadcast information, there is no L1 R2D control information preceding the transmission of MIB-like broadcast information<br>    - M value is a pre-defined value as M=2<br>    - FEC code rate of periodic broadcast information is a pre-defined value<br>    - Repetition number of periodic broadcast information is a pre-defined value<br>  - For SIB1-like broadcast information, M value, FEC code rate and repetition number<br>    - Option 1: are same as MIB-like broadcast information, and there is no L1 R2D control information preceding the transmission of SIB1-like broadcast information<br>    - Option 2: indicated by L1 R2D control information preceding the transmission of SIB1-like broadcast information<br>    - Option 3: indicated by MIB-like broadcast information, and there is no L1 R2D control information preceding the transmission of SIB1-like broadcast information | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602283](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602283.zip) |
| Agreement | For R2D periodic sync signal,<br>- Binary sequence type<br>  - Option 1: m-sequence<br>  - Option 2: Golay sequence<br>- A pre-defined sequence length is in the range from [15 or 31 or 127] to 512<br>  - FFS: detail value<br>  - Note: the value of sequence length refers to the original sequence length with no Manchester coding<br>  - Companies are encouraged to investigate achievable MDR of up to 1% for FAR of up to {1, 10}%<br>- A pre-defined chip length is corresponding to M=2<br>- The number of binary sequence(s) is<br>  - Option 1: 1<br>  - Option 2: one value from {2, 3, 4}<br>- FFS: Whether Manchester coding is applied to binary sequence<br>  - Companies are encouraged to compare performance between with and without Manchester coding under same total transmission time duration | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602283](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602283.zip) |
| Agreement | For chip duration determination of the L1 R2D control information, RAN1 select from the following options:<br>- Option 1: Fixed chip duration. No use of CAP for chip duration determination.<br>- Option 4: Use a set of different binary sequences with fixed or variable length<br>  - Alt.4-1: using binary sequences for SIP. No use of CAP for chip duration determination.<br>  - Alt.4-2: using binary sequences for CAP<br>- Option 5: Use periodic broadcast information to indicate the chip duration | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602284](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602284.zip) |
| Agreement | For R2D, support that unmodulated sinusoid single tone CFO calibration signal is transmitted both periodically and as needed (aperiodically) | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602284](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602284.zip) |
| Agreement | For R2D, for the transmission of periodic unmodulated sinusoid single tone CFO calibration signal<br>- The periodicity is to be selected from the followings<br>  - Option 1: same as the pre-defined transmission periodicity (Psync) of R2D periodic sync signal<br>  - Option 2: integer multiple of Psync<br>- The time location is to be selected from the followings<br>  - Option 4-2: after a periodic R2D sync signal transmission<br>    - Alt1: immediately after the periodic R2D sync signal transmission including padding if any<br>    - Alt2: a gap between the periodic R2D sync signal transmission including padding if any and the CFO calibration signal<br>  - Option 5-2: after a periodic broadcast information transmission(s)<br>    - Alt1: immediately after the periodic broadcast information transmission(s) including padding if any<br>    - Alt2: a gap between the periodic broadcast information transmission including padding if any and the CFO calibration signal | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602284](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602284.zip) |
| Agreement | For R2D, for the transmission of as needed (aperiodic) unmodulated sinusoid single tone CFO calibration signal<br>- The time location is to be selected from the followings<br>  - Option 1: preceding the L1 R2D control information<br>  - Option 2-2: after the data payload of PRDCH at the end of a R2D transmission<br>    - Alt1: immediately after the data payload of PRDCH at the end of a R2D transmission (including padding if any)<br>    - Alt2: a gap between the data payload of PRDCH at the end of a R2D transmission (including padding if any) and the CFO calibration signal<br>  - Option 3: prior to a D2R transmission | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602284](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602284.zip) |
| Working Assumption | For R2D, device assumes that the frequency location of the unmodulated sinusoid single tone CFO calibration signal transmission is within the frequency resource used for the periodic synchronization signal transmission<br>- FFS: detail frequency location | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602285](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602285.zip) |
| Agreement | For R2D, at least following aspects are further considered for the time duration of the unmodulated single tone sinusoid CFO calibration signal transmission,<br>- the time duration and its unit (e.g. millisecond, chip, or OFDM symbol, etc.) of the CFO calibration signal | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602285](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602285.zip) |
| Agreement | For R2D sequence-based SIP,<br>- Binary sequence type<br>  - Option 1: m-sequence<br>  - Option 2: Golay sequence<br>- Pre-defined sequence length(s) in the range from [7 or 15 or 31 or 127] to 512<br>  - Option 1: single length<br>  - Option 2: multiple lengths<br>  - Note: the value of sequence length refers to the original sequence length with no Manchester coding<br>  - Companies are encouraged to investigate achievable MDR of up to 1% for FAR of up to {1, 10}%<br>- Chip length is<br>  - Option 1: pre-defined and corresponding to M=2<br>  - Option 2: determined from multiple M values<br>    - FFS: how to determine<br>- The number of binary sequence(s)<br>  - Option 1: single sequence for a given length<br>  - Option 2: multiple sequences for a given length<br>- FFS: Whether Manchester coding is applied to binary sequence<br>  - Companies are encouraged to compare performance between with and without Manchester coding under same total transmission time duration | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602285](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602285.zip) |
| Agreement | For L1 R2D control information, in addition to chip duration, at least the following transmission parameters for L1 R2D control information are to be determined<br>- For the L1 R2D control information preceding data payload in PRDCH<br>  - Option 1: FEC code rate and repetition number are indicated in periodic broadcast information<br>  - Option 2: FEC code rate is a pre-defined value and repetition number is indicated in periodic broadcast information<br>  - Option 3: repetition number is a pre-defined value and FEC code rate is indicated in periodic broadcast information<br>  - FFS: whether paging can be used to update the transmission parameters or not<br>- For the L1 R2D control information preceding broadcast information in the single-step acquisition (if supported)<br>  - FEC code rate of the L1 R2D control information is a pre-defined value<br>  - Repetition number of the L1 R2D control information is a pre-defined value<br>- For the L1 R2D control information preceding SIB1-like broadcast information in the two-step acquisition (if supported)<br>  - Option 1: FEC code rate and repetition number of the L1 R2D control information are indicated in the MIB-like broadcast information<br>  - Option 2: FEC code rate is a pre-defined value and repetition number is indicated in MIB-like broadcast information<br>  - Option 3: Repetition number is a pre-defined value and FEC code rate is indicated in MIB-like broadcast information<br>  - Option 4: FEC code rate is a pre-defined value and repetition number is a pre-defined value | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2603435](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2603435.zip) |
| Agreement | For CRC attachment of L1 R2D control information, the CRC calculation is to be selected from the followings<br>- Option 1: same as Rel-19<br>- Option 2: always using CRC-16<br>- Option 3: CRC-6 is used for the L1 R2D control information payload up to [56] bits, otherwise CRC-16 is used | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2603435](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2603435.zip) |
| Agreement | For R2D, for interleaving for PRDCH with FEC, RAN1 further select one from the followings:<br>- Alt 1: reuse both LTE sub-block interleaver and LTE bit collection scheme<br>- Alt 2: reuse LTE bit collection scheme without LTE sub-block interleaver<br>- Alt 3: based on LTE bit collection scheme with some update (e.g., for memory reduction) without LTE sub-block interleaver<br>  - Each sub-block is divided into one or two segments according to TB size. For one segment case, it is the same as Alt 2. For two segments case, the bit collection is performed such that 1st segment bits from each sub-block are collected first and then 2nd segment bits from each sub-block are collected<br>- Note: for performance comparison between above alternatives, companies are encouraged to investigate for both small and large TBS, both BLER 10% and 1% | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2603435](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2603435.zip) |
| Agreement | For R2D periodic sync signal, a device may assume that synchronization signal is transmitted with the pre-defined transmission periodicity (Psync)<br>- FFS: whether there is any additional spec impact | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2603435](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2603435.zip) |

## 9.3.2 D2R signals, channels, waveform and procedures

9 Release 20 NR › 9.3 Solutions for Ambient IoT (Internet of Things) in NR Phase 2

| 구분 | 내용 | 출처 |
|---|---|---|
| Agreement | For active devices, D2R line coding is not supported | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx) |
| Agreement | In Rel-20, FEC encoding is performed before block-level repetition for PDRCH. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx) |
| Agreement | For the PDRCH scrambling sequence generator, down-select between the following options:<br>- Option 1: Reuse the NR length-31 Gold sequence generator defined in Section 5.2.1 of TS 38.211<br>- Option 2: Use shorter Gold sequence generator(s) than NR (e.g., length-15 or length-16)<br>- Option 3: Construct the Gold sequence by reusing the D2R preamble m-sequence generators<br>Note: cinit and Nc are discussed separately. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx) |
| Agreement | For Rel-20 D2R preamble, candidate sequence lengths of {7, 31, 63, 127, 255, 511} are considered.<br>- FFS: supported sequence lengths are potentially down-selected from the candidate sequence lengths | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx) |
| Agreement | For Rel-20 D2R midamble, the following insertion structures are considered:<br>- Option 1: the entire generated midamble sequence is inserted at each midamble position (i.e., as in Rel-19)<br>- Option 2: the generated midamble sequence is divided and only part of sequence (e.g., 1 or 2 bit) is inserted at each midamble position<br>- Option 3: A combination of the above options (i.e., both options are used) | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx) |
| Agreement | For PDRCH scrambling sequence initialization, cinit is calculated using at least one of the following:<br>- Option 1: Reader ID-related information<br>- Option 2: Device ID-related information<br>- Option 3: Time-varying information<br>- A combination of two or more of the above options is not precluded. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx) |
| Agreement | For the initial offset Nc of the scrambling sequence generator, down-select between the following:<br>- Option 1: Nc = 1600 as in NR<br>  - FFS: whether additional specification support (e.g., with limiting number of sequences) is needed to reduce the device complexity<br>- Option 2: Nc <1600<br>  - FFS: the specific value of Nc | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx) |
| Agreement | For potential values of D2R bit duration (Tb), the Rel-19 bit duration set {266.67, 133.33, 66.67, 33.33, 16.67, 8.33, 4.17, 1.39} us is used as the starting point, with further discussion on potential down-selection or additional value(s). | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602169](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602169.zip) |
| Agreement | For RAN1 discussion purpose, the D2R transmission bandwidth is 2/Tb.<br>- Note: D2R transmission bandwidth to be specified in RAN4 specification will be discussed in RAN4. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602169](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602169.zip) |
| Agreement | For D2R FDM(A), consider the following two options:<br>- Option 1 (Reference frequency + offset): The D2R frequency resource is determined based on a reference frequency (fref), an offset (foffset) and Tb..<br>- Option 2 (Sub-channel / resource index): The D2R frequency resource is determined based on a sub-channel or resource index.<br>FFS: how the frequency resource is indicated to the device.<br>FFS: Maximum number of FDM(A) transmissions/resources<br>Note: RAN1 expects necessary guard band(s) for D2R FDM(A) are to be determined by RAN4. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602169](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602169.zip) |
| Agreement | For D2R interleaving and bit-collection, down-select from following alternatives<br>- Alt 1: reuse both LTE sub-block interleaver and LTE bit collection scheme<br>- Alt 2: reuse LTE bit collection scheme without LTE sub-block interleaver | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602169](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602169.zip) |
| Agreement | For the maximum number of D2R block-level repetitions, the maximum value is to be down-selected from {4,8,12,16,24,32}. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602169](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602169.zip) |
| Agreement | For D2R preamble sequences with the same length, one of the following options is down-selected:<br>- Option 1: Generation using single polynomial<br>  - The sequences can be generated using different cyclic shift values/initial states.<br>  - FFS: Cyclic shift values or initial states for each sequence<br>- Option 2: Generation using different polynomials<br>  - Option 2-1: Different polynomials with a single cyclic shift value/initial state<br>  - Option 2-2: Different polynomials with multiple cyclic shift values/initial states<br>  - FFS: the polynomials | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602170](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602170.zip) |
| Agreement | Regarding the application of PDRCH scrambling, consider the following options:<br>- Option 1: PDRCH scrambling is applied to all PDRCH transmissions.<br>- Option 2: Whether PDRCH scrambling is applied depends on the PDRCH message type.<br>  - FFS: Message type(s) for which PDRCH scrambling is not applied (e.g., Msg1 in CBRA or Msg1-like for DO-A). | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602170](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602170.zip) |
| Agreement | For Rel-20 D2R midamble, the following details are assumed under the insertion structure Option 1:<br>- Sequence length and type:<br>  - Alt. 1: m-sequence, with sequence lengths to be selected from {3, 7, 15, 31}<br>  - Alt. 2: Hadamard sequence, with sequence lengths to be selected from{2, 4 and other lengths if any}<br>For Rel-20 D2R midamble, the following details are assumed under the insertion structure Option 2:<br>- Inserted length per midamble occasion:<br>  - Alt 1. 1 bit<br>  - Alt 2. 2 bits<br>- Base sequence type and length<br>  - Alt. 1: Same as the preamble (i.e., m-sequence)<br>    - FFS: If the total number of required midamble bits across the PDRCH exceeds the sequence length, the sequence is repeatedly mapped.<br>  - Alt. 2: Hadamard sequence<br>    - FFS: sequence length<br>    - FFS: If the total number of required midamble bits across the PDRCH exceeds the sequence length, the sequence is repeatedly mapped.<br>  - Alt. 3: All 1 sequence<br>    - A sequence consisting entirely of '1's mapped to all required midamble occasions | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602170](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602170.zip) |
| Agreement | For D2R FDM(A), the following details are considered for further discussion under Option 1:<br>- Determination of the reference frequency (fref)<br>  - Alt 1: The reference frequency is indicated by the reader<br>    - Alt 1-1: via periodic broadcast information<br>    - Alt 1-2: via paging message<br>  - Alt 2: The reference frequency is derived from the sync raster point where the device detected the R2D synchronization signal<br>  - Alt 3: The reference frequency is derived from the pre-defined frequency point(s)<br>  - Note: combinations of alternatives are not precluded.<br>- Unit of the offset (foffset):<br>- Alt.1: The offset is represented as an integer multiple of a frequency resource unit (i.e., foffset=k×funit).<br>  - Note: other frequency resource unit may be defined for different purpose<br>  - FFS: funit<br>- Alt.2: The set of offset values is defined and indicated.<br>  - FFS: foffset<br>- Meaning of the offset (foffset):<br>  - Alt.1: The offset indicates the distance from the reference frequency to the center frequency of the D2R transmission.<br>  - Alt 2: The offset indicates the distance from the reference frequency to the lowest frequency of the D2R transmission.<br>- FFS: how to signal foffset<br>For D2R FDM(A), the following details are considered for further discussion under Option 2:<br>- How to derive sub-channels or resource indices:<br>  - A device derives a set of sub-channels or resource indices based on reader-indicated and/or predefined parameters.<br>    - FFS: Necessary information (e.g., transmission bandwidth, the total number of resources/sub-channels, starting frequency of the sub-channels/resources) for deriving the set of sub-channels or resource indices.<br>- The frequency location of each sub-channel is derived based on the sub-channel index and a reference frequency (fref)<br>  - Regarding fref, the alternatives under Option 1 can be considered | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2603449](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2603449.zip) |

| Agreement | Periodic R2D synchronization signal is used by a device for timing acquisition to determine<br>- Start timing for reception of periodic broadcast information<br>- FFS: Other timing information | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602558](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602558.zip) |

| Agreement | For resource allocation for the first D2R transmission for DO-A, support to have multiple D2R resource sets that are periodic or a single D2R resource set provided by a periodic broadcast information.<br>- FFS: modification periodicity of periodic broadcast information at least to indicate the resource allocation information<br>- FFS: details of the D2R resource set<br>  - E.g., the D2R resource set contains one or multiple resource groups that contain one or more resources | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602560](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602560.zip) |
| Agreement | Support at least the following necessary information (i.e., resource allocation related information) determined by device for the first D2R transmission for DO-A<br>- Time domain<br>  - Starting location of the resource set<br>  - Periodicity of the multiple resource sets<br>  - FFS: Number of resource set(s) provided by a periodic broadcast information<br>  - FFS: Number of resource group(s) in each resource set<br>  - FFS: Number of resource(s) in each resource group/set<br>  - Time location of each time domain resource group within each resource set<br>  - Transmission parameters including bit duration, FEC, repetition number<br>- Frequency domain<br>  - Number of FDMA resource(s) in each resource group<br>  - Frequency location of each frequency domain resource<br>- FFS: RSRP threshold(s) for coverage level(s)<br>- FFS: other parameters | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602560](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602560.zip) |
| Agreement | At least support D2R open-loop power control, where device determines the Tx power at least based on R2D RSRP measurement. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602561](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602561.zip) |
| Agreement | Consider following options for determining timing offset value(s) between R2D and corresponding subsequent D2R.<br>- Option 1: provided by a reader<br>- Option 2: pre-defined or derived by pre-defined formula<br>- Combination of above options can also be considered | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602561](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602561.zip) |

| Working Assumption | At least for initial RACH attempt, support determination of total number of PRACH transmissions per RACH attempt as follows:<br>- If a single RSRP threshold is configured, it is used to determine whether to perform multiple PRACH transmissions with different Tx beams, the PRACH transmission number per attempt is equal to or the smallest value of candidate transmission times larger than or the largest value of candidate transmission times smaller than the number of Tx beams UE supports.<br>- If multiple RSRP thresholds are configured, the PRACH transmission number per attempt equals to the each of the values of the total number of multiple PRACH transmissions corresponding to each of the values of multiple RPSP thresholds.<br>- If no RSRP threshold is configured, whether to perform multiple PRACH transmission with different Tx beams and the number of PRACH transmission times is up to UE implementation | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2602217](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2602217.zip) |

9 Release 20 NR › 9.5 Non-Terrestrial Networks (NTN) for NR Phase 4

| Agreement | For the study on GNSS resilient NR-NTN operation, regarding connected mode, at least the following candidate solutions and their combination should be evaluated:<br>- Open-loop time and frequency pre-compensation based on one or more of the following:<br>  - Available and/or applicable assistance information provided by network<br>  - UE location information available at UE<br>  - Satellite ephemeris<br>  - Note: Assistance information provided by network and UE location information available at UE used based on solutions for initial access can be used for connected mode.<br>- TA control, including:<br>  - reuse of legacy closed-loop TA;<br>  - study of enhancements such as extended TAC range, TA drift.<br>- New explicit FAC for connected mode, including:<br>  - explicit indication of instantaneous and/or relative frequency offset;<br>  - possible support of accumulated correction and, if needed, study of optional drift-related parameters.<br>- Overhead and robustness optimization, including:<br>  - combined TAC and FAC indication;<br>  - study of mechanisms to reduce signaling overhead and improve robustness, while noting that group/common adjustment commands require further assessment due to mixed support.<br>- UE reference location update for connected mode (e.g. based on explicit PAC for connected mode)<br>As part of the evaluation of the candidate solutions above, RAN1 should assess at least:<br>- performance, including timing/frequency accuracy and signaling overhead;<br>- applicability to different scenarios, including different orbit types, mobility conditions, cell types, and GNSS outage assumptions;<br>- complexity;<br>- specification impact, including whether the solution remains within the scope of the SID. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx) |

frequency,<br>  - Alt 1-3: two-root transmission with phase shift / PSK-based differentiation by pre-compensation applied to the second sequence,<br>  - Alt 1-4: two-root transmission where the two roots are not necessarily conjugate;<br>- Variant 1A-2: two PRACH sequences using different PRACH formats (e.g. long PRACH followed by short PRACH).<br>- Variant 1A-3: multiple PRACH sequences ## 10.3.1 Channel coding

10 Rel-20 Study of 6GR › 10.3 Channel coding and modulation for 6GR interface

| 구분 | 내용 | 출처 |
|---|---|---|
| Agreement | For the study of LDPC extension beyond NR range, the maximum code block size of 8448 is assumed for further study with the following two options:<br>- Option 1: 44 information columns in BG and a maximum lifting size of 192<br>- Option 2: 22 information columns in BG and a maximum lifting size of 384<br>Note: Aim to decide between option 1 and option 2 during this week. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>`[124b-R20-6GR-Channel coding]` |
| Agreement | For the study of LDPC extension beyond NR range, the mother code rate of approximately 2/3 is assumed for further study.<br>- The above agreement is only applicable for study of the new BG. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>`[124b-R20-6GR-Channel coding]` |
| Agreement | For the study of LDPC extension beyond NR range, for the maximum information block size of 8448 bits, the total number of ones in the lifted parity check matrix under mother code rate is no larger than N, where N is determined by the total number of ones in the lifted parity check matrix of BG1 under the code rate of X.<br>- X is not lower than 1/2.<br>- FFS: Value of X. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>`[124b-R20-6GR-Channel coding]` |
| Agreement | For the study of LDPC extension beyond NR range, for the study of new BG, assume the parity check matrix for encoder consists of five sub-matrices (A, B, C, D, E) for further study, wherein<br>  - Sub-matrix A contains columns that correspond to information bits.<br>  - Sub-matrix C is a zero matrix<br>  - Sub-matrix D: the number of columns of sub-matrix D equals to (the number of columns of Sub-matrix A) + (the number of columns of Sub-matrix B)<br>  - Sub-matrix B contains columns that correspond to core parity check bits<br>  - For sub-matrix B, consider the following options for further study, and strive for down-selection in RAN1#125<br>    - Option B1: A weight-3 column with corresponding shifting values of [0, X, 0] or [X, 0, X], followed by a dual-diagonal structure with corresponding shifting values of [0,0]<br>    - Option B2: At most 2 weight-3 columns and dual-diagonal structure(s) with comparable number of XOR and cyclic shift operations with Option B1, e.g.,<br>        - Option B2-1: Sub-matrix B has a weight-3 column with corresponding shifting values of [a, b, c], followed by a dual-diagonal structure<br>        - Option B2-2: Sub-matrix B can be decomposed into two sets: set 1 and set 2. Each set follows Option B1/ Option B2-1, with additional pair-wise orthogonality across sets, e.g.,<br>  - For sub-matrix E, the following options are identified for further study, and strive for down-selection in RAN1#125<br>    - Option E1: An identity matrix<br>    - Option E2: A lower triangular matrix | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2603340](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2603340.zip)<br>`[124b-R20-6GR-Channel coding]` |
| Agreement | For the study of LDPC extension beyond NR range, for study of the new BG, consider a single edge BG with 44 information columns and a maximum lifting size of 192, with the following options identified for further study, and strive for down-selection among the options in RAN1#125<br>- Option 1: Row splitting, wherein one or more rows outside the core matrix (i.e., sub matrix A and sub matrix B) are split from at least some rows inside the core matrix, and the core column indices of the non-zero entries of the original row are a superset of those of the corresponding split row,<br>    - The core matrix contains Y1 rows, and all elements of Y2 rows in sub-matrix A are ones.<br>        - Y1= 3 or 4<br>        - Y1Y21<br>            - Sub-option 1-1: With at least the first row in sub-matrix A included in the Y2 rows<br>            - Sub-option 1-2: The first row in sub-matrix A is not included in the Y2 rows.<br>    - No puncturing before rate matching<br>- Option 2: The single edge BG with 44 information columns and a maximum lifting size of 192 can be convertible to a BG with 22 information columns and a maximum lifting size of 384. More specifically, each 2x2 submatrix at column 2j, 2j+1, and row 2k, 2k+1 of the BG can only take one of the following forms:<br>, , , .<br>    - No puncturing or puncturing one or two columns before rate matching, wherein the puncturing (if any) is applied within the first two information columns and/or any core parity columns<br>- Option 3: Quasi-row orthogonality in core matrix (i.e., sub matrix A and sub matrix B) and/or sub matrix D, i.e., in which except for certain columns, the row 2k, 2k+1 do not share non-zero entries in the same column in the sub-matrix A and/or sub-matrix D<br>    - No puncturing or puncturing one or two columns before rate matching, wherein the puncturing (if any) is applied within the first two information columns and/or any core parity columns<br>- The options above are not mutually exclusive and can be combined.<br>- Other options that have a single edge BG with 44 information columns and a maximum lifting size of 192 are not precluded. | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>[R1-2603411](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Docs/R1-2603411.zip)<br>`[124b-R20-6GR-Channel coding]` |

| Agreement | For the RAN1 study of “Re-use of existing 5G mid-band (~3.5GHz) site grid for 6G deployments in at least around 7 GHz and targeting comparable coverage to 5G mid-band”, if link budget template candidates 1 is used to calculate the metric(s), the coverage gap between ~7GHz and 5G mid-band is calculated as follows<br>Coverage gap = MPL1 – MPL2 – PL_diff [– additional margin]<br>  - [FFS: detailed value of additional margin]<br>Note:<br>- MPL1: MPL of the evaluated signal/channel in ~7GHz<br>- MPL2: MPL of the bottleneck channel in 5G mid-band (e.g., Rel-15 NR Msg3 for initial/random access procedure)<br>- PL_diff = 20*log10(f1 / f2), where f1 and f2 are the carrier frequency for ~7GHz and 5G mid-band, respectively | [Chair notes RAN1#124bis_eom5](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_124b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23124bis_eom5.docx)<br>`[124b-R20-6GR-General, Synchronization and Beam Measurement]` |
